specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md:31:**结论**：在本次受测版本和有权限读取隔离状态的项目 Hook 环境中，首条消息回调不能依赖标题；下一次自动记录可以按同一会话 ID 读到用户改过的名称，重启后恢复也取得同样结果。`threads.name` 是优先评估的候选，`threads.title` 在改名后仍为旧值，不能用来判断改名。数据库和索引都是本次实测的内部存储，未验证跨版本稳定性，也未确认正式读取方式、读取失败的处理、路径命名或手动目录改名规则。

def run_probe(binary, work, skill_source, link_source, formal=False, extended=False):
    """使用原生信任界面复验正式安装或保留的临时对照。"""
    state = Path(os.environ["CODEX_HOME"]).resolve()
    assert state == work / "state" and Path(os.environ["CODEX_SQLITE_HOME"]).resolve() == state
    repo = work / "repo"
    events = work / "events.jsonl"
    repo.mkdir()
    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q", repo], check=True)
    (repo / "README.md").write_text(README + "\n")
    if formal:
        for arguments in (("config", "user.name", "T035 fixture"), ("config", "user.email", "t035@example.invalid"),
                          ("add", "README.md"), ("commit", "-qm", "fixture baseline")):
            subprocess.run(["git", *arguments], cwd=repo, capture_output=True, check=True)
    target = repo / ".agents/skills"
    maintained = skill_source / "conversation-recording"
    hook_file = repo / ".codex/hooks.json"
    if formal:
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(maintained / "install.py"), "--project", str(repo)],
                       capture_output=True, check=True)
        candidate = target / "conversation-recording/scripts/stop.py"
        prompt_candidate = candidate.with_name("user_prompt_submit.py")
    else:
        subprocess.run(["bash", str(link_source), str(skill_source), str(target)], capture_output=True, check=True)
        hook_file.parent.mkdir()
        candidate = hook_file.parent / "recording-probe.py"
        prompt_candidate = candidate
        shutil.copyfile(__file__, candidate)
        command = shlex.join([sys.executable, str(candidate), "--hook", str(events)])
        hook_file.write_text(json.dumps({"hooks": {event: [{"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command}]}]
                                                    for event in ("UserPromptSubmit", "Stop")}}))
    skill = target / "conversation-recording"
    installed = {str(path.relative_to(skill)): sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                 for path in skill.rglob("*") if path.is_file() and path.name != ".foundry-recording-install.json"}
    base = {str(path.relative_to(maintained)): sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            for path in maintained.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
    assert installed == base and "SKILL.md" in installed, installed
    server = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Provider)
    server.events = events
    server.repo = repo
    server.matrix_work = work
    server.final_gate = threading.Event()
    server.cancel_gate = threading.Event()
    server.write_gate = threading.Event()
    server.partial_start = threading.Event()
    server.partial_finish = threading.Event()
    threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    config = state / "config.toml"
    config.write_text(f"""model = "gpt-5.4"
model_provider = "fixture"
check_for_update_on_startup = false
approval_policy = "never"
sandbox_mode = "{'danger-full-access' if formal else 'read-only'}"
[model_providers.fixture]
name = "T035 isolated"
base_url = "http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/v1"
wire_api = "responses"
requires_openai_auth = false
request_max_retries = 0
stream_max_retries = 0
""")
    expected_images = {}
    user_image = work / "user-image.png"
    if extended:
        mcp_source = Path(__file__).with_name("content-source.py")
        user_image.write_bytes(png_fixture(b"\x42\x23\x11"))
        with config.open("a") as output:
            for label, color in (("alpha", b"\x20\x55\x99"), ("beta", b"\x99\x52\x20")):
                mcp_root = work / f"mcp-{label}"
                source_image = mcp_root / "input/tool-image.png"
                source_image.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
                source_image.write_bytes(png_fixture(color))
                expected_images[label] = sha256(source_image.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                output.write(f"\n[mcp_servers.fixture_{label}]\ncommand = {json.dumps(sys.executable)}\n"
                             f"args = {json.dumps([str(mcp_source), '--mcp', str(mcp_root)])}\n")
        tool_directory = work / "outside-tools"
        tool_directory.mkdir()
        (tool_directory / "evidence.txt").write_text("T035_TOOL_OUTSIDE_BYTES\n")
    tmux = ["tmux", "-f", "/dev/null", "-S", str(work / "terminal.sock")]
    session = "isolated"

    def shell(*args):
        return subprocess.check_output([*tmux, *args], stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)

    def screen():
        try:
            return shell("capture-pane", "-p", "-t", session)
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
            return error.output

    def wait(predicate, label, timeout=15):
        limit = time.monotonic() + timeout
        while time.monotonic() < limit:
            result = predicate()
            if result:
                return result
            time.sleep(0.07)
        raise RuntimeError(f"{label}\n{screen()}\n{trace(events)[-8:]}")

    def enter(value):
        shell("send-keys", "-t", session, "-l", value)
        wait(lambda: value in screen(), "终端已回显输入")
        shell("send-keys", "-t", session, "Enter")

    def start(directory=repo, arguments=(), zone=None):
        command = ("env", f"TZ={zone}", binary) if zone else (binary,)
        shell("new-session", "-d", "-s", session, "-x", "125", "-y", "40", "-c", str(directory),
              *command, *arguments)

    def end():
        enter("/exit")
        wait(lambda: "can't find" in screen() or "no server running" in screen(), "正常退出会话")

    def discovered():
        return asyncio.run(list_hooks(binary, repo, (repo,)))[0]["hooks"]

    def discovered_skill():
        scopes = asyncio.run(discover_project_items(binary, repo, "skills/list", {"cwds": [str(repo)], "forceReload": True}))
        skills = scopes[0]["skills"]
        assert not any(item["name"] == "shadow-profile" for item in skills)
        matching = [item for item in skills if item["name"] == "conversation-recording" and item["enabled"]]
        assert len(matching) == 1 and Path(matching[0]["path"]) == skill / "SKILL.md", matching
        return {"installedProjectSkillDiscovered": True, "retiredEntryCount": 0}

    def read_backs():
        return [event for event in trace(events) if event.get("status") == "read-back"]

    def saved_reply(answer):
    """以只读工作区、独立 CLI 状态、回环网络运行探针。"""
    if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == "--isolated":
        binary, work, source, installer, mode = sys.argv[2:]
        run_probe(binary, Path(work), Path(source), Path(installer), formal=mode in {"formal", "extended"}, extended=mode == "extended")
        return

    workspace = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[5]
    source = workspace / "packages/skills/dist/skill/profile"
    installer = workspace / "scripts/dev/actions/link-skills/link-skills.sh"
    binary = shutil.which("codex")
    assert binary, "需要 Codex CLI 0.172.0"
    version = subprocess.check_output([binary, "--version"], text=True).strip()
    assert version == "codex-cli 0.172.0", version

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="foundry-t035-") as temporary:
        work = Path(temporary).resolve()
        state = work / "state"
        scratch = work / "scratch"
        state.mkdir()
        scratch.mkdir()
        environment = {
            "PATH": "/usr/bin:/bin",
            "TERM": "[已省略环境变量值]",
            "LANG": "C.UTF-8",
            "CODEX_HOME": str(state),
            "CODEX_SQLITE_HOME": str(state),
            "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR": str(state),
            "TMPDIR": str(scratch),
            "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1",
        }
        subprocess.run([
            "/usr/bin/bwrap", "--die-with-parent", "--unshare-user", "--unshare-net", "--unshare-pid",
            "--ro-bind", "/", "/", "--dev", "/dev", "--proc", "/proc", "--tmpfs", "/tmp",
            "--bind", str(work), str(work), "--chdir", str(work), "--",
            sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).resolve()), "--isolated", str(Path(binary).resolve()),
            str(work), str(source), str(installer),
            "extended" if "--extended" in sys.argv else "formal" if "--formal" in sys.argv else "probe",
        ], env=environment, check=True, timeout=230)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def append(kind, **values):
    row = {"kind": kind, "monotonicNs": time.monotonic_ns(), "wallNs": time.time_ns(), **values}
    with LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        stream.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")


def json_lines(path):
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True) if line.endswith(b"\n")]


if sys.argv[1] == "--observe-hook":
    append("Hook", configuredEvent=sys.argv[3], payload=json.load(sys.stdin))

def git(*args):

def tmux(*args):

def screen():

def wait(predicate, description, timeout=15):

    raise RuntimeError(f"{description}\n{screen()[-3500:]}\n{json_lines(LOG)[-5:]}")


def start(*args):
    tmux("new-session", "-d", "-s", "timeline", "-x", "125", "-y", "40", "-c", str(repo), BINARY, *args)


def send(text):
    tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "-l", text)
    wait(lambda: text in screen(), f"终端回显 {text}")
    tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")


def end():
    send("/exit")
    wait(lambda: "can't find" in screen() or "no server running" in screen(),
         "终端正常退出")


def hooks():
    return asyncio.run(list_hooks(BINARY, repo, (repo,)))[0]["hooks"]


def opened_requests(correlation):
    return [item for item in json_lines(LOG) if item["kind"] == "Request"
            and item["correlation"] == correlation and not item["auxiliary"]]


def rollout_row(kind, field, value, thread_id=None):
    for path in (state / "sessions").rglob("rollout-*.jsonl"):
        if thread_id and not path.stem.endswith(thread_id):
            continue
        for number, row in enumerate(json_lines(path), start=1):
            if row.get("payload", {}).get("type") == kind and row["payload"].get(field) == value:
                return {"file": path.name, "line": number, **row}
    return None


def parent_record(turn):


def run():
    repo.mkdir()
    git("init", "-q")
    (repo / "README.md").write_text("隔离合成仓库\n", encoding="utf-8")
    git("add", "README.md")
    git("-c", "user.name=Timeline Fixture", "-c", "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
        "commit", "-qm", "synthetic fixture")
    maintained = SOURCE / "packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording"
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(maintained / "install.py"), "--project", str(repo)],
                   cwd=repo, check=True, capture_output=True)
    installed = repo / ".agents/skills/conversation-recording"
    paths = {path.relative_to(maintained).as_posix(): hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
             for path in maintained.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
    assert paths and all(hashlib.sha256((installed / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest
                         for name, digest in paths.items())
    append("Install", version=subprocess.check_output([BINARY, "--version"], text=True).strip(),
           mode=MODE, hashes=paths)
    hooks_path = repo / ".codex/hooks.json"
    config = json.loads(hooks_path.read_text())
    installed_events = set(config["hooks"])
    assert installed_events == {"UserPromptSubmit", "PostToolUse", "Stop", "SubagentStop"}, installed_events
    for event in EVENTS:
        command = shlex.join([sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--observe-hook", str(WORK), event])
        config["hooks"].setdefault(event, []).append({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command}]})
    hooks_path.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    with http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Endpoint) as server:
        server.gates = {name: threading.Event() for name in ("A", "B", "parent-second")}
        threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        config_path.write_text(f'''model = "gpt-5.4"
model_provider = "fixture"
check_for_update_on_startup = false
approval_policy = "never"
sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"
[features]
multi_agent_v2 = {"true" if MODE == "v2" else "false"}
[model_providers.fixture]
name = "Timeline isolated"
base_url = "http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/v1"
wire_api = "responses"
requires_openai_auth = false
request_max_retries = 0
stream_max_retries = 0
''', encoding="utf-8")
        try:
            assert not hooks()
            start()
            wait(lambda: "Trust and continue" in screen(), "首次项目目录信任", timeout=55)
            time.sleep(0.32)
            tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")
            wait(lambda: "Trust all and continue" in screen(), "首次 Hook 信任")
            candidates = hooks()
            assert candidates and all(entry["trustStatus"] == "untrusted" for entry in candidates)
            tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Down")
            wait(lambda: "› 2. Trust all" in screen(), "临时 Hook 待确认")
            tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")
            wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "临时项目 Codex 就绪")
            trusted = hooks()
            assert len(candidates) == len(trusted)
            assert all(entry["trustStatus"] == "trusted" and entry["enabled"]
                       and Path(entry["sourcePath"]) == hooks_path for entry in trusted), trusted
            append("NativeTrust", hooks=[entry["eventName"] for entry in trusted])
            end()
            assert not records_snapshot(repo), "空白引导会话创建了记录"

            start()
            wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "空白引导后普通启动")
            send(PARENT_PROMPT["first"])
            for label in CHILD_INPUT:
                wait(lambda: opened_requests(label), f"子代理 {label} 已收到合成输入")
            parent = read_back("first")
            assert PARENT_REPLY["first"].split("\n")[-1] in screen()
            wait(lambda: rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", PARENT_REPLY["first"], parent.stem),
                 "父会话完成事件已经持久化")
            assert all(not server.gates[label].is_set() for label in CHILD_INPUT)
            assert all(not rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", text)
                       for text in CHILD_REPLY.values())
            checkpoint("parent-first-done")

            gate_release(server, "A")
            child_finish("A")
            wait(lambda: CHILD_REPLY["A"] in parent.read_text(), "父会话空闲时已写入 A 的实际回复")
            assert all(text in parent.read_text() for text in CHILD_INPUT.values()), parent.read_text()
            assert parent.read_text().index(PARENT_REPLY["first"]) < parent.read_text().index(CHILD_REPLY["A"])
            time.sleep(1.5)
            checkpoint("child-a-parent-idle")

            send(PARENT_PROMPT["second"])
            wait(lambda: opened_requests("second"), "第二轮已抵达父模型，用户原文已进入宿主")
            assert formal_message("发起", PARENT_PROMPT["second"]) in parent.read_text()
            assert not parent_record("second")
            assert not rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", CHILD_REPLY["B"])
            checkpoint("parent-second-started")
            gate_release(server, "B")
            child_finish("B")
            assert CHILD_REPLY["B"] not in parent.read_text()
            append("DeferredUntilSafeParentHook", child="B")
            time.sleep(0.4)
            checkpoint("child-b-parent-second-active")
            gate_release(server, "parent-second")
            assert read_back("second") == parent
            wait(lambda: rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", PARENT_REPLY["second"], parent.stem),

~~~

"""T035 首次启用及首末轮烟雾探针，仅使用隔离项目、合成回复和临时记录器。"""

def log(path, **data):
    with path.open("a") as output:
        output.write(json.dumps({"ns": time.monotonic_ns(), "wallNs": time.time_ns(), **data}) + "\n")


def trace(path):
    return f"<!-- foundry-message {label} {len(text)} -->\n\n### {title}\n\n{text}\n"


def records_snapshot(repo):
    """检查合成项目的记录写入和字节变化。"""
    roots = (repo / "records", repo / "conversation-recording/records")
    return {path.relative_to(repo).as_posix(): [path.stat().st_mtime_ns, sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()]
            for root in roots for path in root.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}


def png_fixture(color):
    """生成一个合成原件，供两处同名附件的字节回查。"""
    def chunk(kind, payload):
        return struct.pack(">I", len(payload)) + kind + payload + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(kind + payload))

    header = struct.pack(">2I5B", 1, 1, 8, 2, 0, 0, 0)
    return b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" + chunk(b"IHDR", header) + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(b"\0" + color)) + chunk(b"IEND", b"")


class Provider(HostResponses):
    """回环端点仅返回合成回复或一次读取 README 的工具请求。"""

    def matrix_tool(self, outputs, available):
        """驱动真实 MCP 文件工具以及包含完整参数的命令和续流。"""
        call = next((name for name in MATRIX_TOOL_IDS if name not in outputs), None)
        if not call:
            return None
        if call.startswith("matrix-image-"):
            label = call.removeprefix("matrix-image-")
            tool = next(t for t in available if f"fixture_{label}" in t.get("name", ""))
            if tool.get("tools"):
                nested = next(t for t in tool["tools"] if t["name"] == "attachment")
                return self.call(call, nested["name"], {"label": label}, tool["name"])
            return self.call(call, tool["name"], {"label": label})
        if call == "matrix-poll":
            match = re.search(r"session ID (\d+)", str(outputs["matrix-stream"]))
            assert match, outputs["matrix-stream"]
            return self.call(call, "write_stdin", {"session_id": int(match[1]), "chars": "", "yield_time_ms": 3000})
        commands = {
            "matrix-outside": ("/usr/bin/cat evidence.txt", str(self.server.matrix_work / "outside-tools"), 1000, 2000),
            "matrix-long": (f"python3 -c 'print(\"{LONG_HEAD}\"); print(\"repeat abc 123\" * 700); print(\"{LONG_TAIL}\")'",
                            str(self.server.repo), 1000, 12000),
            "matrix-stream": ("printf 'T035_STREAM_FIRST\\n'; sleep 2; printf 'T035_STREAM_LAST\\n'",
                              str(self.server.repo), 1000, 2000),
        }
        cmd, workdir, yield_time, limit = commands[call]
        return self.call(call, "exec_command", {"cmd": cmd, "workdir": workdir,
                                                  "yield_time_ms": yield_time, "max_output_tokens": limit})

    @staticmethod
    def call(call, name, arguments, namespace=None):
        item = {"type": "function_call", "id": f"fc-{call}", "call_id": call,
                "name": name, "arguments": json.dumps(arguments, ensure_ascii=False)}
        if namespace:
            item["namespace"] = namespace
        return item

    def do_POST(self):
        request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))))
        last_user = next((item for item in reversed(request["input"]) if item.get("role") == "user"), {})
        user_text = json.dumps(last_user, ensure_ascii=False)
        is_aux = "text" in request
        correlation = "unrecognized"
        resume_answer = next((answer for answer in RESUMES if answer in user_text), None)
        if MATRIX_PROMPT in user_text:
            correlation = "matrix"
        elif OUTSIDE_PROMPT in user_text:
            correlation = "outside"
        elif FOREIGN_PROMPT in user_text:
            correlation = "foreign"
        elif PAUSED_PROMPT in user_text:
            correlation = "interrupted"
        elif CONTINUED in user_text:
            correlation = "continued"

~~~

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_d8dc0e8df6154df9a3f3e69a14af6feb`

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const d=load("title_probe_root");
const p=String.raw`cat > PROBE_DIRECTORY/title_probe.py <<'PY'
"""在临时项目内观察真实模型的首轮命名派发与主回复时序。"""

import asyncio
from hashlib import sha256
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import runpy
import shlex
import subprocess
import sys
import time

WORK = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = WORK / "repo"
STATE = WORK / "state"
BINARY = WORK / "codex-package/bin/codex"
TRACE = WORK / "observations.jsonl"
RELEASE = WORK / "release-title"
SESSION = "foundry-title"
PROMPT = "雨后为什么容易看到彩虹？请用一句话回答。"
MARKER = "FOUNDRY_TITLE_DISPATCH_PROBE"
EVENTS = ("PostToolUse", "SubagentStart", "SubagentStop", "Stop")


def observe(kind, **values):
    """只写本次合成会话的必要观测字段。"""
    row = {"kind": kind, "ns": time.monotonic_ns(), **values}
    with TRACE.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as output:
        output.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")


def rows(path=TRACE):
    """只返回已完整写入的事件。"""
    if not path.is_file():
        return []
    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True) if line.endswith(b"\n")]


def formal(label, value):
    heading = "用户 · 发起" if label == "发起" else "主代理 · 回复"
    return f"<!-- foundry-message {label} {len(value)} -->\n\n### {heading}\n\n{value}\n"


def record_for(session):
    """只定位此合成会话应写入的本地文件。"""
    return next((path for root in (REPO / "records", REPO / "conversation-recording/records")
                 for path in root.glob(f"*/{session}.md") if path.is_file()), None)


def add_naming_instruction():
    """先执行正式安装脚本，读回原话后才追加派发指令。"""
    contents = sys.stdin.buffer.read()
    data = json.loads(contents)
    installed = REPO / ".agents/skills/conversation-recording/scripts/user_prompt_submit.py"
    previous = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(installed)], input=contents, cwd=REPO, capture_output=True)
    if previous.returncode or previous.stdout or data.get("prompt") != PROMPT:
        observe("RecorderDiagnostic", returncode=previous.returncode, producedOutput=bool(previous.stdout))
        sys.stdout.buffer.write(previous.stdout)
        raise SystemExit(previous.returncode)
    record = record_for(data["session_id"])
    assert record and formal("发起", PROMPT) in record.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    gate = shlex.join([sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--await-release"])
    delegated = (f"{MARKER}。你是本会话的命名子代理。以下是用户已落盘的原话：{PROMPT}\n"
                 "先执行这个只读命令来等待隔离探针放行："
                 f"{gate}\n命令结束后，按原话判断主题，输出 JSON 对象，只含 title 字段。"
                 "主题明确就填一个简短中文标题，主题不明确就填 null。")
    hint = ("【项目的会话记录命名工作流】本轮用户原话已保存。请先调用 Codex 原生子代理工具，"
            "创建一个名为 recording_title 的命名子代理，把下面的完整任务文本作为 message 传给它。"
            "若工具支持 fork_context，请设为 false。创建后直接正常回答用户的问题。"
            "不等待、不查询命名子代理的结果，主回复只回答原问题。\n\n" + delegated)
    observe("OriginalSavedBeforeHint", session=data["session_id"], turn=data["turn_id"], prompt=PROMPT,
            record=record.relative_to(REPO).as_posix(), savedSha256=sha256(record.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
            additionalContextSha256=sha256(hint.encode()).hexdigest())
    print(json.dumps({"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": "UserPromptSubmit", "additionalContext": hint}},
                     ensure_ascii=False))


def observe_hook(event):
    """保留身份、真实工具和发言，跳过无关 payload。"""
    data = json.load(sys.stdin)
    values = {key: data[key] for key in ("session_id", "turn_id", "agent_id", "agent_type", "tool_use_id",
                                         "tool_name", "last_assistant_message", "model", "stop_hook_active")
              if key in data}
    if event == "PostToolUse":
        values["probeMarkerInInput"] = MARKER in json.dumps(data.get("tool_input"), ensure_ascii=False)
    if event == "SubagentStop" and data.get("agent_transcript_path"):
        values["agentLogName"] = Path(data["agent_transcript_path"]).name
    observe(event, **values)
    print("{}")


def await_release():
    """从子代理的只读工具等待主回复确认。"""
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 85
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if RELEASE.is_file():
            print("FOUNDRY_TITLE_GATE_OPEN")
            return
        time.sleep(0.07)
    raise SystemExit("命名结果等待超时")


def git(*arguments):
    subprocess.run(["git", *arguments], cwd=REPO, check=True, capture_output=True)


def mux(*arguments):
    return subprocess.check_output(["tmux", "-f", "/dev/null", "-S", str(WORK / "terminal.sock"), *arguments],
                                   stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)


def screen():
    try:
        return mux("capture-pane", "-p", "-S", "-110", "-t", SESSION)
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
        return error.output


def wait(predicate, label, timeout=30):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        result = predicate()
        if result:
            return result
        time.sleep(0.075)
    raise TimeoutError(label)


def start():
    mux("new-session", "-d", "-s", SESSION, "-x", "114", "-y", "32", "-c", str(REPO),
        str(BINARY), "--no-alt-screen")


def send(value):
    mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "-l", value)
    wait(lambda: value in screen(), "终端输入回显")
    mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Enter")


def end():
    send("/exit")
    wait(lambda: "can't find" in screen() or "no server running" in screen(), "终端退出")


def log_for(session):
    """只读取由本次探针 Hook 取得的会话 ID。"""
    log = next((p for p in (STATE / "sessions").rglob("rollout-*.jsonl") if p.stem.endswith(session)), None)
    return rows(log) if log else []


def normalize_visible(value):
    return re.sub(r"[\s*_`]", "", value)


def finals(session):
    selected = []
    for row in log_for(session):
        payload = row.get("payload", {})
        if (row["type"] == "response_item" and payload.get("type") == "message"
                and payload.get("role") == "assistant" and payload.get("phase") == "final_answer"):
            body = "".join(part["text"] for part in payload["content"] if part["type"] == "output_text")
            selected.append({"timestamp": row["timestamp"], "body": body})
    return selected


def hook_row(event, session):
    return next((row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == event and row.get("session_id") == session), None)


def run():
    REPO.mkdir()
    git("init", "-q")
    (REPO / "README.md").write_text("命名派发隔离样例。\n", encoding="utf-8")
    git("add", "README.md")
    git("-c", "user.name=Foundry Fixture", "-c", "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
        "commit", "-qm", "fixture baseline")

    maintained = WORK / "source"
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(maintained / "install.py"), "--project", REPO],
                   cwd=REPO, check=True, capture_output=True)
    installed = REPO / ".agents/skills/conversation-recording"
    checked = {p.relative_to(maintained).as_posix(): sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
               for p in maintained.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    assert all(sha256((installed / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == expected for name, expected in checked.items())
    observe("Install", cli=subprocess.check_output([BINARY, "--version"], text=True).strip(),
            installedFiles=len(checked), sourceHashes=checked)
    hook_file = REPO / ".codex/hooks.json"
    config = json.loads(hook_file.read_text())
    assert set(config["hooks"]) == {"UserPromptSubmit", "PostToolUse", "SubagentStop", "Stop"}
    config["hooks"]["UserPromptSubmit"][0]["hooks"][0]["command"] = shlex.join(
        [sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--submit"])
    for event in EVENTS:
        command = shlex.join([sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--observe", event])
        config["hooks"].setdefault(event, []).append({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command}]})
    hook_file.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    (STATE / "config.toml").write_text(
        'model = "gpt-6.2-sol"\nmodel_reasoning_effort = "medium"\n'
        'check_for_update_on_startup = false\napproval_policy = "never"\nsandbox_mode = "read-only"\n'
        'cli_auth_credentials_store = "file"\n[features]\nmulti_agent_v2 = true\n'
        'multi_agent_v2_dynamic_tools = false\n', encoding="utf-8")
    tools = runpy.run_path(str(WORK / "helpers/recording-hook-probe.py"))
    def discovered():
        return asyncio.run(tools["list_hooks"](str(BINARY), REPO, (REPO,)))[0]["hooks"]

    login = subprocess.run([BINARY, "login", "status"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert login.returncode == 0 and "ChatGPT" in login.stdout + login.stderr
    observe("NativeLogin", via="现有原生登录只读挂载")
    try:
        assert not discovered()
        start()
        wait(lambda: "Trust and continue" in screen(), "首次临时项目目录信任", timeout=60)
        time.sleep(0.32)
        mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Enter")
        wait(lambda: "Trust all and continue" in screen(), "首次 Hook 信任")
        untrusted = discovered()
        assert untrusted and all(entry["trustStatus"] == "untrusted" for entry in untrusted)
        mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Down")
        wait(lambda: "› 2. Trust all" in screen(), "选择临时 Hook 信任")
        mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Enter")
        wait(lambda: "gpt-6.2-sol" in screen() and "Trust all and continue" not in screen(),
             "原生启动就绪", timeout=45)
        trusted = discovered()
        assert len(trusted) == len(untrusted)
        assert all(entry["trustStatus"] == "trusted" and entry["enabled"]
                   and Path(entry["sourcePath"]) == hook_file for entry in trusted)
        observe("NativeTrust", hooks=[entry["eventName"] for entry in trusted])
        end()
        assert not list((REPO / "records").rglob("*.md"))

        start()
        wait(lambda: "gpt-6.2-sol" in screen(), "普通终端已启动", timeout=45)
        send(PROMPT)
        committed = wait(lambda: next((row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == "OriginalSavedBeforeHint"), None),
                         "原话提交后写入及读回")
        session = committed["session"]
        turn = committed["turn"]
        observe("UserInputVerified", session=session, turn=turn)

        def start_or_stop():
            return hook_row("SubagentStart", session) or hook_row("Stop", session)
        event = wait(start_or_stop, "真实模型派发子代理或直接完成父回复", timeout=100)
        if event["kind"] != "SubagentStart":
            observe("Outcome", status="真实模型在本用例未派发", parent=session, turn=turn)
            raise AssertionError("真实模型在本用例未派发")
        child = event["agent_id"]
        stop = wait(lambda: hook_row("Stop", session), "父回复结束", timeout=85)
        root_reply = stop["last_assistant_message"]
        assert root_reply and "彩虹" in root_reply
        record = record_for(session)
        assert record
        wait(lambda: formal("回复", root_reply) in record.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
             "实发主回复已写入并读回")
        wait(lambda: normalize_visible(root_reply) in normalize_visible(screen()),
             "终端已呈现实际主回复")
        main_final = finals(session)
        assert len(main_final) == 1 and main_final[0]["body"] == root_reply
        before_child = not hook_row("SubagentStop", session) and not finals(child)
        observe("ParentReadBack", section=session, turn=turn, visible=True, reply=root_reply,
                record=record.relative_to(REPO).as_posix(), resultStillPending=bool(before_child),
                hostAt=main_final[0]["timestamp"])
        (WORK / "parent-before-release.screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")

        observe("ReleaseGate", child=child)
        RELEASE.touch()
        child_stop = wait(lambda: hook_row("SubagentStop", session), "读取原生命名子代理回传", timeout=75)
        assert child_stop["agent_id"] == child and child_stop["last_assistant_message"]
        try:
            result = json.loads(child_stop["last_assistant_message"])
        except ValueError:
            observe("Outcome", status="命名结果格式不符", parent=session, child=child)
            raise
        assert isinstance(result, dict) and set(result) == {"title"} and isinstance(result["title"], str)
        assert result["title"].strip() and "彩虹" in result["title"]
        child_final = wait(lambda: finals(child), "子会话实发消息落盘")
        assert len(child_final) == 1 and child_final[0]["body"] == child_stop["last_assistant_message"]
        child_events = log_for(child)
        metadata = next(row["payload"] for row in child_events if row["type"] == "session_meta")
        assert metadata["id"] == child
        relation = metadata["source"]["subagent"]["thread_spawn"]
        assert relation["parent_thread_id"] == session
        invitations = [row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == "PostToolUse" and row.get("session_id") == session
                       and row.get("probeMarkerInInput") and str(row.get("tool_name")).endswith("spawn_agent")]
        assert len(invitations) == 1
        gate_call = any(row["payload"].get("type") == "function_call"
                        and "--await-release" in str(row["payload"].get("arguments")) for row in child_events)
        gate_call = gate_call or any(row["payload"].get("type") in {"custom_tool_call", "function_call"}
                                     and "--await-release" in json.dumps(row["payload"]) for row in child_events)
        time.sleep(0.65)
        assert len(finals(session)) == 1 and record_for(session) == record
        assert before_child and main_final[0]["timestamp"] < child_final[0]["timestamp"]
        observed_readback = next(row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == "ParentReadBack")
        assert committed["ns"] < event["ns"] <= observed_readback["ns"] < child_stop["ns"]
        assert not any(row["kind"] == "PostToolUse" and row.get("session_id") == session
                       and str(row.get("tool_name")).endswith("wait_agent") for row in rows())
        assert "Blocked by hook" not in screen()
        observe("Outcome", status="通过", parent=session, child=child, turn=turn, title=result["title"],
                parentReplyAt=main_final[0]["timestamp"], childReplyAt=child_final[0]["timestamp"],
                hasChildGateCall=bool(gate_call), normalReplyCount=len(finals(session)),
                childParentVerified=True, finalDirectoryUnchanged=record.relative_to(REPO).as_posix())
        (WORK / "after-child.screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")
        end()
        print(json.dumps({"status": "通过", "title": result["title"], "model": stop.get("model"),
                          "parentReplyAt": main_final[0]["timestamp"], "childReplyAt": child_final[0]["timestamp"],
                          "childGateCall": gate_call}, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
    except BaseException as error:
        observe("Diagnostic", stage=type(error).__name__)
        (WORK / "probe-last.screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")
        if isinstance(error, TimeoutError):
            print(str(error), file=sys.stderr)
        raise
    finally:
        if not RELEASE.exists():
            RELEASE.touch()
        subprocess.run(["tmux", "-f", "/dev/null", "-S", str(WORK / "terminal.sock"), "kill-server"],
                       capture_output=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    mode = sys.argv[1]
    if mode == "--submit":
        add_naming_instruction()
    elif mode == "--observe":
        observe_hook(sys.argv[2])
    elif mode == "--await-release":
        await_release()
    elif mode == "--run":
        run()
    else:
        raise ValueError(mode)
PY

python3 -B - PROBE_DIRECTORY/title_probe.py <<'PY'
import sys
from pathlib import Path
probe = Path(sys.argv[1])
compile(probe.read_bytes(), str(probe), "exec")
PY`;

{

const d=load("title_probe_root");
const p=String.raw`cat > PROBE_DIRECTORY/title_probe.py <<'PY'
"""观测普通首轮经 Hook 派发命名子代理的实际时序。"""

import asyncio
from hashlib import sha256
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import runpy
import shlex
import subprocess
import sys
import time

WORK = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = WORK / "repo"
STATE = WORK / "state"
BINARY = WORK / "codex-package/bin/codex"
TRACE = WORK / "observations.jsonl"
RELEASE = WORK / "release-title"
SESSION = "foundry-title"
PROMPT = "雨后为什么容易看到彩虹？请用一句话回答。"
MARKER = "FOUNDRY_TITLE_DISPATCH_PROBE"
EVENTS = ("PostToolUse", "SubagentStart", "SubagentStop", "Stop")


def observe(kind, **values):
    row = {"kind": kind, "ns": time.monotonic_ns(), **values}
    with TRACE.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as output:
        output.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")


def rows(path=TRACE):
    if not path.is_file():
        return []
    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True) if line.endswith(b"\n")]


def formal(label, value):
    heading = "用户 · 发起" if label == "发起" else "主代理 · 回复"
    return f"<!-- foundry-message {label} {len(value)} -->\n\n### {heading}\n\n{value}\n"


def record_for(session):
    return next((path for root in (REPO / "records", REPO / "conversation-recording/records")
                 for path in root.glob(f"*/{session}.md") if path.is_file()), None)


def add_naming_instruction():
    """先执行正式安装脚本，读回原话后才追加派发指令。"""
    contents = sys.stdin.buffer.read()
    data = json.loads(contents)
    installed = REPO / ".agents/skills/conversation-recording/scripts/user_prompt_submit.py"
    previous = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(installed)], input=contents, cwd=REPO, capture_output=True)
    if previous.returncode or previous.stdout or data.get("prompt") != PROMPT:
        observe("RecorderDiagnostic", returncode=previous.returncode, producedOutput=bool(previous.stdout))
        sys.stdout.buffer.write(previous.stdout)
        raise SystemExit(previous.returncode)
    record = record_for(data["session_id"])
    assert record and formal("发起", PROMPT) in record.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    gate = shlex.join([sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--await-release"])
    delegated = (f"{MARKER}。你是本会话的命名子代理。以下是用户已落盘的原话：{PROMPT}\n"
                 "先执行这个只读命令来等待隔离探针放行："
                 f"{gate}\n命令结束后，按原话判断主题，输出 JSON 对象，只含 title 字段。"
                 "主题明确就填一个简短中文标题，主题不明确就填 null。")
    hint = ("【项目的会话记录命名工作流】本轮用户原话已保存。请先调用 Codex 原生子代理工具，"
            "创建一个名为 recording_title 的命名子代理，把下面的完整任务文本作为 message 传给它。"
            "若工具支持 fork_context，请设为 false。创建后直接正常回答用户的问题。"
            "不等待、不查询命名子代理的结果，主回复只回答原问题。\n\n" + delegated)
    observe("OriginalSavedBeforeHint", session=data["session_id"], turn=data["turn_id"], prompt=PROMPT,
            record=record.relative_to(REPO).as_posix(), savedSha256=sha256(record.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
            additionalContextSha256=sha256(hint.encode()).hexdigest())
    print(json.dumps({"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": "UserPromptSubmit", "additionalContext": hint}},
                     ensure_ascii=False))


def observe_hook(event):
    """只保留本次合成运行的必要字段。"""
    data = json.load(sys.stdin)
    values = {key: data[key] for key in ("session_id", "turn_id", "agent_id", "agent_type", "tool_use_id",
                                         "tool_name", "last_assistant_message", "model", "stop_hook_active")
              if key in data}
    if event == "PostToolUse":
        values["probeMarkerInInput"] = MARKER in json.dumps(data.get("tool_input"), ensure_ascii=False)
    if event == "SubagentStop" and data.get("agent_transcript_path"):
        values["agentLogName"] = Path(data["agent_transcript_path"]).name
    observe(event, **values)
    print("{}")


def await_release():
    """从子代理工具等待主回复读回。"""
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 85
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if RELEASE.is_file():
            print("FOUNDRY_TITLE_GATE_OPEN")
            return
        time.sleep(0.07)
    raise SystemExit("命名结果等待超时")


def git(*arguments):
    subprocess.run(["git", *arguments], cwd=REPO, check=True, capture_output=True)


def mux(*arguments):
    return subprocess.check_output(["tmux", "-f", "/dev/null", "-S", str(WORK / "terminal.sock"), *arguments],
                                   stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)


def screen():
    try:
        return mux("capture-pane", "-p", "-S", "-110", "-t", SESSION)
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
        return error.output


def wait(predicate, label, timeout=30):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        result = predicate()
        if result:
            return result
        time.sleep(0.075)
    raise TimeoutError(label)


def start():
    mux("new-session", "-d", "-s", SESSION, "-x", "114", "-y", "32", "-c", str(REPO),
        str(BINARY), "--no-alt-screen")


def send(value):
    mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "-l", value)
    wait(lambda: value in screen(), "终端输入回显")
    mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Enter")


def end():
    send("/exit")
    wait(lambda: "can't find" in screen() or "no server running" in screen(), "终端退出")


def log_for(session):
    """按已观察到的会话 ID 读取探针宿主日志。"""
    log = next((p for p in (STATE / "sessions").rglob("rollout-*.jsonl") if p.stem.endswith(session)), None)
    return rows(log) if log else []


def normalize_visible(value):
    return re.sub(r"[\s*_\x60]", "", value)


def finals(session):
    selected = []
    for row in log_for(session):
        payload = row.get("payload", {})
        if (row["type"] == "response_item" and payload.get("type") == "message"
                and payload.get("role") == "assistant" and payload.get("phase") == "final_answer"):
            body = "".join(part["text"] for part in payload["content"] if part["type"] == "output_text")
            selected.append({"timestamp": row["timestamp"], "body": body})
    return selected


def hook_row(event, session):
    return next((row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == event and row.get("session_id") == session), None)


def run():
    REPO.mkdir()
    git("init", "-q")
    (REPO / "README.md").write_text("命名派发隔离样例。\n", encoding="utf-8")
    git("add", "README.md")
    git("-c", "user.name=Foundry Fixture", "-c", "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
        "commit", "-qm", "fixture baseline")

    maintained = WORK / "source"
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(maintained / "install.py"), "--project", REPO],
                   cwd=REPO, check=True, capture_output=True)
    installed = REPO / ".agents/skills/conversation-recording"
    checked = {p.relative_to(maintained).as_posix(): sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
               for p in maintained.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    assert all(sha256((installed / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == expected for name, expected in checked.items())
    observe("Install", cli=subprocess.check_output([BINARY, "--version"], text=True).strip(),
            installedFiles=len(checked), sourceHashes=checked)
    hook_file = REPO / ".codex/hooks.json"
    config = json.loads(hook_file.read_text())
    assert set(config["hooks"]) == {"UserPromptSubmit", "PostToolUse", "SubagentStop", "Stop"}
    config["hooks"]["UserPromptSubmit"][0]["hooks"][0]["command"] = shlex.join(
        [sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--submit"])
    for event in EVENTS:
        command = shlex.join([sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--observe", event])
        config["hooks"].setdefault(event, []).append({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command}]})
    hook_file.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    (STATE / "config.toml").write_text(
        'model = "gpt-6.2-sol"\nmodel_reasoning_effort = "medium"\n'
        'check_for_update_on_startup = false\napproval_policy = "never"\nsandbox_mode = "read-only"\n'
        'cli_auth_credentials_store = "file"\n[features]\nmulti_agent_v2 = true\n'
        'multi_agent_v2_dynamic_tools = false\n', encoding="utf-8")
    tools = runpy.run_path(str(WORK / "helpers/recording-hook-probe.py"))

    def discovered():
        return asyncio.run(tools["list_hooks"](str(BINARY), REPO, (REPO,)))[0]["hooks"]

    login = subprocess.run([BINARY, "login", "status"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert login.returncode == 0 and "ChatGPT" in login.stdout + login.stderr
    observe("NativeLogin", via="现有原生登录只读挂载")
    try:
        assert not discovered()
        start()
        wait(lambda: "Trust and continue" in screen(), "首次临时项目目录信任", timeout=60)
        time.sleep(0.32)
        mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Enter")
        wait(lambda: "Trust all and continue" in screen(), "首次 Hook 信任")
        untrusted = discovered()
        assert untrusted and all(entry["trustStatus"] == "untrusted" for entry in untrusted)
        mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Down")
        wait(lambda: "› 2. Trust all" in screen(), "选择临时 Hook 信任")
        mux("send-keys", "-t", SESSION, "Enter")
        wait(lambda: "gpt-6.2-sol" in screen() and "Trust all and continue" not in screen(),
             "原生启动就绪", timeout=45)
        trusted = discovered()
        assert len(trusted) == len(untrusted)
        assert all(entry["trustStatus"] == "trusted" and entry["enabled"]
                   and Path(entry["sourcePath"]) == hook_file for entry in trusted)
        observe("NativeTrust", hooks=[entry["eventName"] for entry in trusted])
        end()
        assert not list((REPO / "records").rglob("*.md"))

        start()
        wait(lambda: "gpt-6.2-sol" in screen(), "普通终端已启动", timeout=45)
        send(PROMPT)
        committed = wait(lambda: next((row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == "OriginalSavedBeforeHint"), None),
                         "原话提交后写入及读回")
        session = committed["session"]
        turn = committed["turn"]
        observe("UserInputVerified", session=session, turn=turn)

        def start_or_stop():
            return hook_row("SubagentStart", session) or hook_row("Stop", session)

        event = wait(start_or_stop, "真实模型派发子代理或直接完成父回复", timeout=100)
        if event["kind"] != "SubagentStart":
            observe("Outcome", status="真实模型在本用例未派发", parent=session, turn=turn)
            raise AssertionError("真实模型在本用例未派发")
        child = event["agent_id"]
        stop = wait(lambda: hook_row("Stop", session), "父回复结束", timeout=85)
        root_reply = stop["last_assistant_message"]
        assert root_reply
        record = record_for(session)
        assert record
        wait(lambda: formal("回复", root_reply) in record.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
             "实发主回复已写入并读回")
        wait(lambda: normalize_visible(root_reply) in normalize_visible(screen()),
             "终端已呈现实际主回复")
        main_final = finals(session)
        assert len(main_final) == 1 and main_final[0]["body"] == root_reply
        before_child = not hook_row("SubagentStop", session) and not finals(child)
        observe("ParentReadBack", parent=session, turn=turn, visible=True, reply=root_reply,
                record=record.relative_to(REPO).as_posix(), resultStillPending=bool(before_child),
                hostAt=main_final[0]["timestamp"])
        (WORK / "parent-before-release.screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")

        observe("ReleaseGate", child=child)
        RELEASE.touch()
        child_stop = wait(lambda: hook_row("SubagentStop", session), "读取原生命名子代理回传", timeout=75)
        assert child_stop["agent_id"] == child and child_stop["last_assistant_message"]
        result = json.loads(child_stop["last_assistant_message"])
        assert isinstance(result, dict) and set(result) == {"title"} and isinstance(result["title"], str)
        assert result["title"].strip() and "彩虹" in result["title"]
        child_final = wait(lambda: finals(child), "子会话实发消息落盘")
        assert len(child_final) == 1 and child_final[0]["body"] == child_stop["last_assistant_message"]
        child_events = log_for(child)
        metadata = next(row["payload"] for row in child_events if row["type"] == "session_meta")
        assert metadata["id"] == child
        relation = metadata["source"]["subagent"]["thread_spawn"]
        assert relation["parent_thread_id"] == session
        invitations = [row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == "PostToolUse" and row.get("session_id") == session
                       and row.get("probeMarkerInInput") and str(row.get("tool_name")).endswith("spawn_agent")]
        assert len(invitations) == 1
        gate_call = any(row["payload"].get("type") in {"custom_tool_call", "function_call"}
                        and "--await-release" in json.dumps(row["payload"]) for row in child_events)
        time.sleep(0.65)
        assert len(finals(session)) == 1 and record_for(session) == record
        assert before_child and main_final[0]["timestamp"] < child_final[0]["timestamp"]
        readback = next(row for row in rows() if row["kind"] == "ParentReadBack")
        assert committed["ns"] < event["ns"] <= readback["ns"] < child_stop["ns"]
        assert not any(row["kind"] == "PostToolUse" and row.get("session_id") == session
                       and str(row.get("tool_name")).endswith("wait_agent") for row in rows())
        assert "Blocked by hook" not in screen()
        observe("Outcome", status="通过", parent=session, child=child, turn=turn, title=result["title"],
                parentReplyAt=main_final[0]["timestamp"], childReplyAt=child_final[0]["timestamp"],
                hasChildGateCall=bool(gate_call), normalReplyCount=len(finals(session)),
                childParentVerified=True, finalDirectory=record.relative_to(REPO).as_posix())
        (WORK / "after-child.screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")
        end()
        print(json.dumps({"status": "通过", "title": result["title"], "model": stop.get("model"),
                          "parentReplyAt": main_final[0]["timestamp"], "childReplyAt": child_final[0]["timestamp"],
                          "childGateCall": gate_call}, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
    except BaseException as error:
        observe("Diagnostic", stage=type(error).__name__)
        (WORK / "probe-last.screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")
        raise
    finally:
        if not RELEASE.exists():
            RELEASE.touch()
        subprocess.run(["tmux", "-f", "/dev/null", "-S", str(WORK / "terminal.sock"), "kill-server"],
                       capture_output=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    mode = sys.argv[1]
    if mode == "--submit":
        add_naming_instruction()
    elif mode == "--observe":
        observe_hook(sys.argv[2])
    elif mode == "--await-release":
        await_release()
    elif mode == "--run":
        run()
    else:
        raise ValueError(mode)
PY
mkdir -p PROBE_DIRECTORY/state PROBE_DIRECTORY/home PROBE_DIRECTORY/scratch PROBE_DIRECTORY/codex-package PROBE_DIRECTORY/source PROBE_DIRECTORY/helpers
python3 -B - PROBE_DIRECTORY/title_probe.py <<'PY'

from pathlib import Path
probe = Path(sys.argv[1])
compile(probe.read_bytes(), str(probe), "exec")
PY`;
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:p.replaceAll("PROBE_DIRECTORY",d),max_output_tokens:1400});text(r);

## 工具结果 `exec-eb9ae3dc-81a1-49fa-b650-5929dcc52058`

## 真实模型交互核查（2026-11-23）

按用户回复「开始吧」继续 [T004 真实模型验证方案](../implementation/T004.md#真实模型验证方案2026-11-23)。使用 `codex-cli 0.171.0` 和真实 `gpt-6.2-sol`，以 `--no-daemon --no-alt-screen` 启动交互伪终端。Bubblewrap 绑定 T002 临时仓库、独立 CLI 状态及仓库外资料区，现有登录由 CLI 读取只读挂载。候选 Skill 临时安装在样例仓库的 `.agents/skills/t004-live-recording/SKILL.md`，工具用 `workspace-write` 与资料区的附加写权限。项目和用户 hook 关闭。测试提示只含合成任务。

第一轮交互启动停在临时仓库的信任确认，随后在隔离配置中确认该仓库后重试。初版候选在普通任务中被模型主动完整读取。该轮未从工具输出取得可确认的 CLI 父进程，随后只执行了业务读取。增加祖先进程观测后，同样的权限设置下首次保存成功，模型在一次工具调用中先保存再执行了业务读取。再要求读取确认和业务调用分开后，在仓库根和子目录取得独立调用的证据。三个候选快照及工具轨迹见 [合成观测](acceptance/t004-live/observations.json)。

### 首条、末轮与范围对照

| 场景 | 实际结果 |
| --- | --- |
| 仓库根普通交互 | 空白期间未新增记录。模型收到「读一下 README.md，告诉我第一行」，自行读取候选正文。独立工具调用先保存并读回用户原文和索引，再读取 `README.md`。正常退出后，实际末轮「对话记录合成基线」与详情保存原文一致。 |
| 子目录跨午夜 | 会话在本地 11 月 22 日 23:59 开启，11 月 23 日发送首条。文件名信号和成对记录使用 11 月 22 日。首条读回早于独立业务读取，实际末轮与保存原文一致。 |
| 仓库内正常恢复 | 保留同一会话标识，沿用 11 月 22 日的成对文件。详情新增一轮，原索引仍指向该详情。 |
| 主动停止 | 发出包含 `sleep 45` 的普通任务，在工具执行时按 Ctrl+C。宿主记录 `turn_aborted`，原因为 `interrupted`。停止前没有该会话的记录，未发出普通末轮。 |
| 同名前缀仓库外目录 | 模型执行了仓库外样例的 README 读取，工具轨迹中没有仓库内候选读取。记录文件新增、续写均为零。 |
| 仓库内 `exec` | 真实完成普通任务，记录文件新增、续写均为零。会话元数据的 `source` 为 `exec`，工具祖先进程输出未显示 `exec` 参数。 |
| 仓库内 `exec resume` | 沿用原交互会话标识，实际续写一份详情，违反非交互零写入要求。 |
| 仓库内恢复仓库外会话 | 选择当前目录进入仓库内。模型只读取原会话首行的 `session_meta`，输出其中仓库外的原始目录。记录文件新增、续写均为零。 |
| 仓库外恢复仓库内会话 | 选择历史目录进入仓库内。工具显示的 `codex` 进程目录也是仓库内，模型将其作为启动来源并续写一份详情。实际启动目录在仓库外，违反跨边界零写入要求。 |

对照采用文件哈希和 Inotify 事件。观察中有宿主沙箱创建并清除 `.git`、`.agents`、`.codex` 和 `.aws` 临时目录的事件。合成观测单独列出这类目录事件及实际记录文件事件。范围外会话的工具轨迹没有读取对话正文，仓库内恢复仓库外样例的会话文件读取限于无缓冲首行元数据。

### 进程信号复核

从仓库外恢复仓库内会话并选择历史目录，使用单独合成请求核对进程参数。工具可见的 PID 1 的 `comm` 为 `codex`，`argv[0]` 为 `codex-linux-sandbox`，带有 `--command-cwd` 和 `--apply-seccomp-then-exec`。它的工作目录为恢复后的仓库内目录。这解释了为何按进程名匹配会误把沙箱进程认作 CLI 启动器。

报告证据并\\n55:回到方案调整路线，只阻止受影响下游，不无理由冻结无关工作。通过的验证实现 MUST 保留为后续增量\\n56:骨架，不推倒重写；技术验证通过后仍须按业务条件验收，不自动完成业务闭环。\\n63:Task 范围列表；技术假设与验证依据可引用相关材料。已有 plan 缺\\n70:### IV. 最小充分验证\\n72:工作流验收按复杂度和失败影响选择：小型验证单项规则或功能，中型验证一个完整用户流程，大型验证\\n82:不自动进入主要实现、其他模式或下游阶段；发现范围或验收需要改变时先说明影响并取得对应确认。\\n86:阶段顺序、模式分工及确认点 MUST 以当前有效规格、方案、任务和用户明确授权为依据；技术依赖通过\\n88:未完成项；要求真实用户确认、实践或学习证据时，MUST 保留尚未满足的条件，不用 agent 演练替代。\\n99:真正开工前 MUST 有适用且经真实讨论认可、成功保存并读回的实现方案。自然语言实施与显式\\n101:项目既有约定、方案及证据；无有效方案先讨论。用户可提供方向，AI 也可基于项目提议，经真实\\n105:任务链接。写清必要目标、逻辑与数据关系、理由、顺序、验收、讨论来源和调整依据即可，不设\\n107:安排可以在已确认逻辑内合理调整并留依据，逻辑/职责/范围/验收的实质变化先停受影响部分重议。\\n110:每个实际进入业务实施的 Task 都须完整消费共同准备并核对自身有效方案、确认范围、保存结果、\\n113:只做允许的核查、讨论或修复，不推进受影响业务。此为 agent 执行约定，不宣称程序锁或全聊天拦截。\\n115:治理修订、独立资料核查和隔离候选验证须明确范围及授权，不把尚未交付的实施能力设为无输入\\n116:依赖的资料工作前置，也不把准备动作当业务实施授权。候选验证须保留实际来源；候选成功不等于\\n117:最终来源已安装/消费。最终来源变化后须刷新隔离正式入口，核对版本、完整正文、必要附件及\\n122:基础、讲解、用户实践、反馈及掌握验证，默认用户主写，局部代写不扩大授权。工程/学习/报告\\n126:分工、有效授权、确认与待定事项及下一动作，不重复实施、不串用其他 Task 的状态、不静默改验收。\\n128:续接必须复用已确认结论：先核对用户已确认的修复、最终保留代码及已撤回方案。记录与代码不一致时\\n141:旧任务。清理在需求实现及必要验证完成后、整体结案前执行；任务 MUST 明确对象位置、处置依据和\\n147:当前语义与引用；单次文本搜索无命中不能作为可删除依据。用途无法确认的内容 MUST 保留，记录\\n151:对象身份或状态系统。其余应清理内容处理完毕、必要验证及引用检查通过后，清理部分才可判完成；\\n152:存在未处理应清理项、保存失败、必要验证失败或引用受损时不得标记完成，按既有恢复规则处理，\\n160:`apps/`、`packages/` 前缀；用户未明确指定包时，按需求描述推断并向用户确认一次后再建目录。NNN\\n168:Spec 内资料按职责安排，原生主文件不搬移：原始输入默认 requirements/raw，讨论与确认\\n\"}"

`--no-daemon --no-alt-screen` 启动交互伪终端。Bubblewrap 绑定 T002 临时仓库、独立 CLI 状态及仓库外资料区，现有登录由 CLI 读取只读挂载。候选 Skill 临时安装在样例仓库的 `.agents/skills/t004-live-recording/SKILL.md`，工具用 `workspace-write` 与资料区的附加写权限。项目和用户 hook 关闭。测试提示只含合成任务。\\n\\n第一轮交互启动停在临时仓库的信任确认，随后在隔离配置中确认该仓库后重试。初版候选在普通任务中被模型主动完整读取。该轮未从工具输出取得可确认的 CLI 父进程，随后只执行了业务读取。增加祖先进程观测后，同样的权限设置下首次保存成功，模型在一次工具调用中先保存再执行了业务读取。再要求读取确认和业务调用分开后，在仓库根和子目录取得独立调用的证据。三个候选快照及工具轨迹见 [合成观测](acceptance/t004-live/observations.json)。\\n\\n### 首条、末轮与范围对照\\n\\n| 场景 | 实际结果 |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| 仓库根普通交互 | 空白期间未新增记录。模型收到「读一下 README.md，告诉我第一行」，自行读取候选正文。独立工具调用先保存并读回用户原文和索引，再读取 `README.md`。正常退出后，实际末轮「对话记录合成基线」与详情保存原文一致。 |\\n| 子目录跨午夜 | 会话在本地 11 月 22 日 23:59 开启，11 月 23 日发送首条。文件名信号和成对记录使用 11 月 22 日。首条读回早于独立业务读取，实际末轮与保存原文一致。 |\\n| 仓库内正常恢复 | 保留同一会话标识，沿用 11 月 22 日的成对文件。详情新增一轮，原索引仍指向该详情。 |\\n| 主动停止 | 发出包含 `sleep 45` 的普通任务，在工具执行时按 Ctrl+C。宿主记录 `turn_aborted`，原因为 临时目录的事件。合成观测单独列出这类目录事件及实际记录文件事件。范围外会话的工具轨迹没有读取对话正文，仓库内恢复仓库外样例的会话文件读取限于无缓冲首行元数据。\\n\\n### 进程信号复核\\n\\n从仓库外恢复仓库内会话并选择历史目录，使用单独合成请求核对进程参数。工具可见的 PID 1 的 `comm` 为 `codex`，`argv[0]` 为 source=source.replace('    wait(lambda: "can\'t find" in screen() or "no server running" in screen(), "终端退出")',
'''    wait(lambda: mux("display-message", "-p", "-t", SESSION, "#{pane_dead}").strip() == "1", "终端退出")
    mux("kill-session", "-t", SESSION)''')
start=source.index('def run():\n')
end=source.index('    tools = runpy.run_path(', start)
block=source[start+len('def run():\n'):end]
source=source[:start]+'def run(reuse=False):\n    if not reuse:\n'+indent(block,'    ')+\
       '    hook_file = REPO / ".codex/hooks.json"\n'+source[end:]
start=source.index('        assert not discovered()\n')
end=source.index('        assert not list((REPO / "records").rglob("*.md"))',start)
body=source[start:end]
source=source[:start]+'        if not reuse:\n'+indent(body,'    ')+'''
        else:
            entries = discovered()
            assert entries and all(entry["trustStatus"] == "trusted" and entry["enabled"]
                                   and Path(entry["sourcePath"]) == hook_file for entry in entries)
            observe("NativeTrust", hooks=[entry["eventName"] for entry in entries], reused=True)
'''+source[end:]
source=source.replace('    elif mode == "--run":\n        run()', '    elif mode == "--run":\n        run(reuse=True)')
assert 'run(reuse=True)' in source and '"--no-daemon"' in source
compile(source,str(script),"exec")
script.write_text(source)
(work/'tmux.conf').write_text('set -g remain-on-exit on\n')
PY`;
const cmd=clone.replaceAll("FIRST_DIRECTORY",first);
const ready=await tools.exec_command({cmd,max_output_tokens:1200});text(ready);if(ready.exit_code!==0)exit();
const newest=load("title_probe_root");
const launch=load("title_probe_launch_template").replaceAll(newest,first);
store("title_probe_root",first);store("title_probe_launch_template",launch);
const job=await tools.exec_command({cmd:launch,yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:2100});
if(job.session_id)store("title_live_session",job.session_id);text(job);

~~~

## 工具结果 `exec-11829314-6136-458f-9a92-ea408c00dae7`

- 来源定位：字节 9865515

~~~text
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md:33:## 2026-11-26 首轮结束后派发命名子代理的隔离对照
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md:35:本次验证 [子代理首次命名需求](../raw/2026-11-26-recording-initial-title-by-subagent.md) 的技术前置，不涉及 Codex 界面主动改名。使用 Linux Codex CLI `0.172.0`、两个临时 Git 仓库及独立宿主状态，沿用上面的网络隔离和本地回环夹具。从维护源安装现有记录 Skill，通过原生信任后普通重开。两组都只发一条关于密码重置界面的合成请求；首段回答由回环端点固定返回 `PROBE_FIRST_REPLY`，不调用业务工具。仅在临时项目加一个 `Stop` 探针，对照是否返回继续决定。继续组由受控回包选择宿主真实的 `spawn_agent` 和 `wait_agent`；不以该回包证明真实模型会自行选择工具。
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:92:- 用户确认自动命名只采纳一次：新会话先用会话 ID 建档；第一轮完整问答结束后，主题明确时由子代理根据已保存的早期对话生成短标题，主题不明确时继续用会话 ID。采纳短标题后，后续对话不再触发命名。此确认不改变已约定的主动改名和同名后缀规则。 [用户原话及确认](requirements/raw/2026-11-26-recording-initial-title-by-subagent.md)
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:98:- [NEEDS CLARIFICATION: 标题含不适合作为路径的字符时如何命名？首次自动命名采用子代理；普通问答未调用模型工具时，自动派发命名子代理仍待技术验证。Codex 界面主动改名的名称读取另见[标题来源调研](requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md)。]
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:128:11. 给定已用会话 ID 临时建档的会话，第一轮完整问答结束后，主题明确时由子代理根据已保存的早期对话生成短标题。在原开启日目录下用采纳的标题重命名会话目录，并同步更新受影响的记录链接；有详情与附件时，主记录仍能打开对应详情，详情仍能打开原附件。后续对话即使换了主题也不再次派发命名，恢复时在现有目录续写，不另建主记录。
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:131:14. 给定第一轮完整问答未形成明确主题，或未取得可采纳的子代理标题，继续使用会话 ID 目录，正常保存回复和后续对话，不因等待命名而停写。
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:202:- **FR-003** 记录流程在实际保存时根据项目规则和已核实的会话位置选择或复核资料根。首次选址依次考虑项目自定义规则、Agent 工具的实际项目内资料目录、根据项目情况自选的项目内记录目录。主记录、各组详情文件和会话附件均纳入项目 Git 管理，随项目变更提交，并遵守[详情文件与附件](#详情文件与附件)中的提交边界。Git 忽略规则不得覆盖新写入的记录或附件。每个会话按实际开启的本地日期建档；新增布局在日期目录下按对话标题命名会话目录，目录内包含主记录、详情和附件。首次记录以会话 ID 临时命名会话目录，不等待标题，按 FR-002 保存并读回首条消息。第一轮完整问答结束后，主题明确且已保存时，由命名子代理根据已保存的早期对话生成短标题。采纳标题后，在原日期目录下用标题重命名会话目录，并同步更新受影响的记录链接，保留已保存内容及会话 ID。主题不明确或未取得可采纳的标题时，保留会话 ID 目录并继续记录；命名不阻塞正常记录。成功采纳后不因后续对话变化再次自动派发命名，仍适用同名后缀和用户在 Codex 界面主动改名的规则。同一日期目录下，对话标题相同时，所有同名会话的目录名均在标题后添加彼此可区分的短后缀，包括先建档的会话；改名时同步更新受影响的记录链接，不覆盖任何记录。本次澄清不迁移或改写既有记录。会话 ID、实际开启时间和首次工作目录继续作为现有身份及归属核验材料，是否调整其保存方式待目录方案讨论。连续交互和正常恢复跨日均沿合规的资料根和原开启日续写同一份主记录；目录已改名时使用新路径。并行会话各自保存。此次开发变更完成时，移除旧索引版生成的记录产物，建档和续写统一采用经确认的新规则。

~~~

ID。采纳短标题后，后续对话不再触发命名。此确认不改变已约定的主动改名和同名后缀规则。 [用户原话及确认](requirements/raw/2026-11-26-recording-initial-title-by-subagent.md)\nspecs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:98:- [NEEDS CLARIFICATION: 标题含不适合作为路径的字符时如何命名？首次自动命名采用子代理；普通问答未调用模型工具时，自动派发命名子代理仍待技术验证。Codex 界面主动改名的名称读取另见[标题来源调研](requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md)。]\nspecs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:128:11. 给定已用会话 ID 临时建档的会话，第一轮完整问答结束后，主题明确时由子代理根据已保存的早期对话生成短标题。在原开启日目录下用采纳的标题重命名会话目录，并同步更新受影响的记录链接；有详情与附件时，主记录仍能打开对应详情，详情仍能打开原附件。后续对话即使换了主题也不再次派发命名，恢复时在现有目录续写，不另建主记录。\nspecs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:131:14. 给定第一轮完整问答未形成明确主题，或未取得可采纳的子代理标题，继续使用会话 ID 目录，正常保存回复和后续对话，不因等待命名而停写。\nspecs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:202:- **FR-003** 记录流程在实际保存时根据项目规则和已核实的会话位置选择或复核资料根。首次选址依次考虑项目自定义规则、Agent 工具的实际项目内资料目录、根据项目情况自选的项目内记录目录。主记录、各组详情文件和会话附件均纳入项目 Git 管理，随项目变更提交，并遵守[详情文件与附件](#详情文件与附件)中的提交边界。Git 忽略规则不得覆盖新写入的记录或附件。每个会话按实际开启的本地日期建档；新增布局在日期目录下按对话标题命名会话目录，目录内包含主记录、详情和附件。首次记录以会话 ID 临时命名会话目录，不等待标题，按 FR-002 保存并读回首条消息。第一轮完整问答结束后，主题明确且已保存时，由命名子代理根据已保存的早期对话生成短标题。采纳标题后，在原日期目录下用标题重命名会话目录，并同步更新受影响的记录链接，保留已保存内容及会话 ID。主题不明确或未取得可采纳的标题时，保留会话 ID 目录并继续记录；命名不阻塞正常记录。成功采纳后不因后续对话变化再次自动派发命名，仍适用同名后缀和用户在 Codex 界面主动改名的规则。同一日期目录下，对话标题相同时，所有同名会话的目录名均在标题后添加彼此可区分的短后缀，包括先建档的会话；改名时同步更新受影响的记录链接，不覆盖任何记录。本次澄清不迁移或改写既有记录。会话 ID、实际开启时间和首次工作目录继续作为现有身份及归属核验材料，是否调整其保存方式待目录方案讨论。连续交互和正常恢复跨日均沿合规的资料根和原开启日续写同一份主记录；目录已改名时使用新路径。并行会话各自保存。此次开发变更完成时，移除旧索引版生成的记录产物，建档和续写统一采用经确认的新规则。\n"
  }
]
~~~

from pathlib import Path

for e in events:
 if e['kind'] in {'OriginalSavedBeforeHint','PostToolUse','SubagentStart','Stop','ParentReadBack','ReleaseGate','SubagentStop'}:
  print(json.dumps(e,ensure_ascii=False))
parent=next(e['session'] for e in events if e['kind']=='OriginalSavedBeforeHint')

 print(label,x)
 for row in map(json.loads,p.read_text().splitlines()):
  a=row['payload']
  k=a.get('type')

   print('来源',a.get('source'))
  if k in {'function_call','custom_tool_call'}:

   print(row['timestamp'],k,(a.get('item') or {}).get('agent_path'),a.get('phase'))
PY`;

"""以宿主的真实父子身份核对命名探针，导出合成结果。"""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from hashlib import sha256
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
import tomllib

work, current_source = map(Path, sys.argv[1:3])
observed = [json.loads(line) for line in (work / "observations.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]


def exact(items, predicate):
    matches = [item for item in items if predicate(item)]
    assert len(matches) == 1, len(matches)
    return matches[0]


def hook(event, **fields):
    return exact(observed, lambda row: row["kind"] == event and all(row.get(key) == value for key, value in fields.items()))


def get_rollout(session):
    path = exact(list((work / "state/sessions").rglob("rollout-*.jsonl")),
                 lambda file: file.stem.endswith(session))
    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_bytes().splitlines()]


def final_answer(events):
    event = exact(events, lambda row: row["type"] == "response_item"
                  and row["payload"].get("type") == "message"
                  and row["payload"].get("role") == "assistant" and row["payload"].get("phase") == "final_answer")
    text = "".join(part["text"] for part in event["payload"]["content"] if part["type"] == "output_text")
    return event["timestamp"], text


def body_appears_in_tui(text, screen):
    def normalize(text):
        return re.sub(r"[\s*_\x60]", "", text)
    return normalize(text) in normalize(screen)


def timestamp(value):
    return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))


def after_ms(row):
    return round((row["ns"] - saved["ns"]) / 1e6, 3)


installed = hook("Install")
trusted = hook("NativeTrust")
assert {"userPromptSubmit", "postToolUse", "subagentStart", "subagentStop", "stop"} == set(trusted["hooks"])
hashes = installed["sourceHashes"]
assert len(hashes) == installed["installedFiles"] == 20
actual = {path.relative_to(current_source).as_posix(): sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
          for path in current_source.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
assert actual == hashes
installed_copy = work / "repo/.agents/skills/conversation-recording"
assert all(sha256((installed_copy / path).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == expected for path, expected in hashes.items())
with (work / "state/config.toml").open("rb") as settings:
    config = tomllib.load(settings)
assert config["features"]["multi_agent_v2"] and "model_providers" not in config
assert config["model"] == "gpt-6.2-sol" and config["model_reasoning_effort"] == "medium"

saved = hook("OriginalSavedBeforeHint")
parent_id, parent_turn = saved["session"], saved["turn"]
parent_rows = get_rollout(parent_id)
root_stop = hook("Stop", session_id=parent_id, turn_id=parent_turn)
started = hook("SubagentStart", session_id=parent_id)
child_id, child_turn = started["agent_id"], started["turn_id"]
child_rows = get_rollout(child_id)
child_stop = hook("SubagentStop", session_id=parent_id, turn_id=child_turn, agent_id=child_id)
parent_tool = hook("PostToolUse", session_id=parent_id, turn_id=parent_turn, tool_name="collaborationspawn_agent")
assert root_stop["model"] == started["model"] == child_stop["model"] == config["model"]
assert not root_stop["stop_hook_active"] and not child_stop["stop_hook_active"]
root_info = exact(parent_rows, lambda row: row["type"] == "session_meta")["payload"]
assert root_info["source"] == "cli" and root_info["id"] == parent_id
child_meta = exact(child_rows, lambda row: row["type"] == "session_meta")["payload"]
assert child_meta["id"] == child_id
relation = child_meta["source"]["subagent"]["thread_spawn"]
assert relation["parent_thread_id"] == parent_id and relation["depth"] == 1

dispatch = exact(parent_rows, lambda row: row["type"] == "response_item"
                 and row["payload"].get("type") == "function_call"
                 and row["payload"].get("name") == "spawn_agent"
                 and row["payload"].get("call_id") == parent_tool["tool_use_id"])
arguments = json.loads(dispatch["payload"]["arguments"])
assert arguments["task_name"] == "recording_title" and arguments["fork_turns"] == "none"
steps = [row for row in parent_rows if row["type"] == "event_msg"
         and row["payload"].get("type") == "item_completed"
         and (row["payload"].get("item") or {}).get("type") == "SubAgentActivity"]
start_item = exact(steps, lambda row: row["payload"]["item"].get("kind") == "started")
item = start_item["payload"]["item"]
assert (start_item["payload"]["turn_id"], item["id"], item["agent_thread_id"], item["agent_path"]) == (
    parent_turn, parent_tool["tool_use_id"], child_id, relation["agent_path"])
assert relation["agent_path"] == "/root/recording_title"
assert "FOUNDRY_TITLE_DISPATCH_PROBE" not in arguments.get("message", "")
assert not parent_tool["probeMarkerInInput"]

readback = hook("ParentReadBack", parent=parent_id, turn=parent_turn)
release = hook("ReleaseGate", child=child_id)
parent_at, parent_text = final_answer(parent_rows)
child_at, child_text = final_answer(child_rows)
assert (parent_at, parent_text) == (readback["hostAt"], readback["reply"])
assert parent_text == root_stop["last_assistant_message"]
assert child_text == child_stop["last_assistant_message"]
title = json.loads(child_text)
assert set(title) == {"title"} and isinstance(title["title"], str) and "彩虹" in title["title"]
before_return = (work / "parent-before-release.screen.txt").read_text()
assert readback["visible"] and readback["resultStillPending"]
assert body_appears_in_tui(parent_text, before_return) and "Blocked by hook" not in before_return
assert saved["ns"] < parent_tool["ns"] < readback["ns"] < release["ns"] < child_stop["ns"]
assert timestamp(dispatch["timestamp"]) <= timestamp(parent_at) < timestamp(child_at)
gate = exact(child_rows, lambda row: row["type"] == "response_item"
             and row["payload"].get("type") in {"custom_tool_call", "function_call"}
             and "--await-release" in json.dumps(row["payload"]))
gate_result = exact(child_rows, lambda row: row["type"] == "response_item"
                    and row["payload"].get("type") in {"custom_tool_call_output", "function_call_output"}
                    and "FOUNDRY_TITLE_GATE_OPEN" in json.dumps(row["payload"]))
assert gate["payload"]["call_id"] == gate_result["payload"]["call_id"]
assert timestamp(parent_at) < timestamp(gate["timestamp"]) < timestamp(child_at)
assert not any(row["type"] == "response_item" and row["payload"].get("name") == "wait_agent" for row in parent_rows)
assert not any(row["type"] == "response_item" and row["payload"].get("type") in {"custom_tool_call", "function_call"}
               and row["payload"].get("name") != "spawn_agent" for row in parent_rows)
record = work / "repo" / saved["record"]
assert record.stem == parent_id and saved["prompt"] in record.read_text() and parent_text in record.read_text()
developer_hint = any(row["type"] == "response_item" and row["payload"].get("type") == "message"
                     and row["payload"].get("role") == "developer"
                     and "FOUNDRY_TITLE_DISPATCH_PROBE" in json.dumps(row["payload"]) for row in parent_rows)

evidence = {
    "date": timestamp(parent_at).astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=8))).date().isoformat(),
    "cli": installed["cli"],
    "model": config["model"],
    "reasoningEffort": config["model_reasoning_effort"],
    "modelBackend": "Codex 原生登录的真实模型",
    "installedFiles": hashes,
    "trustedProbeEvents": sorted(set(trusted["hooks"])),
    "user": {"prompt": saved["prompt"], "parentId": parent_id, "turnId": parent_turn,
             "record": saved["record"], "initialReadBackSha256": saved["savedSha256"],
             "additionalContextSha256": saved["additionalContextSha256"],
             "hintFoundInDeveloperRolloutMessage": developer_hint},
    "dispatch": {"at": dispatch["timestamp"], "tool": "collaboration.spawn_agent",
                 "callId": parent_tool["tool_use_id"], "taskName": arguments["task_name"],
                 "agentPath": relation["agent_path"], "childId": child_id, "childTurn": child_turn,
                 "postToolUseMsFromReadBack": after_ms(parent_tool),
                 "plainMarkerFoundInToolPayload": False},
    "parentReply": {"at": parent_at, "text": parent_text, "readBackMsFromOriginal": after_ms(readback),
                    "visibleBeforeChildResult": readback["visible"] and readback["resultStillPending"],
                    "finalAnswerCount": 1, "waitAgentCalls": 0},
    "naming": {"at": child_at, "result": title, "hookMsFromOriginal": after_ms(child_stop),
               "secondsAfterParentReply": (timestamp(child_at) - timestamp(parent_at)).total_seconds(),
               "gateToolCalledAt": gate["timestamp"], "gateWasOpenOnToolInvocation": True,
               "callbackAcceptedAfterGate": child_stop["ns"] > release["ns"]},
    "probeChecks": {"completedRealModelSamples": 1, "sameParentChildVerified": True,
                    "initialProbeAssertion": "旧断言从派发输入匹配明文标记失败",
                    "finalVerification": "用宿主任务名、工具调用、子线程身份及原生回调重新核对通过",
                    "directoryRenameExercised": False},
}
print(json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
PY
/usr/bin/python3 -B PROBE_DIRECTORY/audit_title_probe.py PROBE_DIRECTORY packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording > PROBE_DIRECTORY/verified-title-dispatch.json`;
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:p.replaceAll("PROBE_DIRECTORY",d),max_output_tokens:2500});text(r);

const d=load("title_probe_root");
const q=String.raw`set -e
python3 - PROBE_DIRECTORY/audit_title_probe.py <<'PY'
import sys
from pathlib import Path
p=Path(sys.argv[1])
content=p.read_text()
old='''developer_hint = any(row["type"] == "response_item" and row["payload"].get("type") == "message"
                     and row["payload"].get("role") == "developer"
                     and "FOUNDRY_TITLE_DISPATCH_PROBE" in json.dumps(row["payload"]) for row in parent_rows)'''
new='''developer_hint = exact(parent_rows, lambda row: row["type"] == "response_item"
                       and row["payload"].get("type") == "message"
                       and row["payload"].get("role") == "developer"
                       and "FOUNDRY_TITLE_DISPATCH_PROBE" in json.dumps(row["payload"]))
assert timestamp(developer_hint["timestamp"]) < timestamp(dispatch["timestamp"])'''
assert content.count(old)==1
content=content.replace(old,new)
content=content.replace('"hintFoundInDeveloperRolloutMessage": developer_hint', '"hintInDeveloperRolloutAt": developer_hint["timestamp"]')
p.write_text(content)
PY
/usr/bin/python3 -B PROBE_DIRECTORY/audit_title_probe.py PROBE_DIRECTORY packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording > PROBE_DIRECTORY/verified-title-dispatch.json
python3 - PROBE_DIRECTORY/verified-title-dispatch.json <<'PY'
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
source=Path(sys.argv[1])
contents=source.read_bytes()
evidence=json.loads(contents)
assert evidence['parentReply']['visibleBeforeChildResult']
assert evidence['naming']['secondsAfterParentReply']==18.01
destination=Path('specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/acceptance/recording-title-first-prompt.json')
with destination.open('xb') as output:
    output.write(contents)
print(destination)
PY`;

{
  "command": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md\n@@\n **结论**：在首段回复零工具调用的前提下，`Stop` 可让宿主重新进入同一父轮，并在受控条件下派发和取得真实子线程回传。但这不是正常结束后独立、无干扰的命名：它延续原轮次，产生用户可见输出，且现有记录器没有保存第二段回复。不能把该路线作为首期自动命名的验收通过。建议保留普通 `Stop` 的落盘职责，先评估能否在父轮结束后独立派发；若无合适入口，再讨论延后命名。未运行真实模型自主派发、独立后台派发、主题判定、失败重试、目录改名或链接更新；未修改当前工作区安装、Hook、规格或任务。\n+\n+## 2026-11-27 首条落盘后派发命名子代理\n+\n+用户确认首条原话保存后即可尝试命名。命名子代理根据已保存的早期发言返回标题或空值。先验证派发与正常回复可以独立完成，再接入目录改名。 [用户确认](../raw/2026-11-27-recording-title-trigger.md)\n+\n+使用 Linux Codex CLI 0.172.0 和真实 `gpt-6.2-sol`，推理力度为 medium。临时 Git 仓库从维护源运行 `install.py` 并逐一核对 20 个安装文件。临时 CLI 只读使用现有原生登录，项目和 Hook 通过原生信任流程启用。探针在临时 `UserPromptSubmit` 里调用正式保存脚本，确认原话「雨后为什么容易看到彩虹？请用一句话回答。」已经写入主记录，再通过 `additionalContext` 给父模型追加派发指令。宿主实际在开发者消息中载入该指令。 [观测投影](../../analysis/acceptance/recording-title-first-prompt.json)\n+\n+- 父模型调用一次原生 `collaboration.spawn_agent`，子线程是 `/root/recording_title`。父会话发出一条正常回复，终端实发和主记录原文一致，当时命名子代理尚未返回。\n+- 子代理在主回复之后 18.010 秒发出 `{\"title\":\"雨后为什么容易看到彩虹\"}`。原生 `SubagentStop` 回传与子轮实发内容一致。父线程、派发调用 ID、子线程与轮次均已核对。探针的等待工具调用发生在放行之后，时序结论来自实际观察到的先后。\n+- 本次派发正文在宿主输入中以非明文形式记录。初版探针按输入内容匹配标记时断言失败，读回改按宿主任务名、子线程 ID 和原生父子关系核验。正式接入需沿同样的可核实身份关联命名回调。\n+\n+本次只覆盖一个主题明确的合成首轮。空标题、重试、目录重命名和链接更新留待各自验收。临时项目仍使用会话 ID 主记录路径。 [时序、回传及安装文件校验](../../analysis/acceptance/recording-title-first-prompt.json)\n*** End Patch"
}
~~~

+
+## 2026-11-27 首条落盘后派发命名子代理
+
+用户确认首条原话保存后即可尝试命名。命名子代理根据已保存的早期发言返回标题或空值。先验证派发与正常回复可以独立完成，再接入目录改名。 [用户确认](../raw/2026-11-27-recording-title-trigger.md)
+
+使用 Linux Codex CLI 0.172.0 和真实 \`gpt-6.2-sol\`，推理力度为 medium。临时 Git 仓库从维护源运行 \`install.py\` 并逐一核对 20 个安装文件。临时 CLI 只读使用现有原生登录，项目和 Hook 通过原生信任流程启用。探针在临时 \`UserPromptSubmit\` 里调用正式保存脚本，确认原话「雨后为什么容易看到彩虹？请用一句话回答。」已经写入主记录，再通过 \`additionalContext\` 给父模型追加派发指令。宿主实际在开发者消息中载入该指令。 [观测投影](../../analysis/acceptance/recording-title-first-prompt.json)
+
+- 父模型调用一次原生 \`collaboration.spawn_agent\`，子线程是 \`/root/recording_title\`。父会话发出一条正常回复，终端实发和主记录原文一致，当时命名子代理尚未返回。
+- 子代理在主回复之后 18.010 秒发出 \`{"title":"雨后为什么容易看到彩虹"}\`。原生 \`SubagentStop\` 回传与子轮实发内容一致。父线程、派发调用 ID、子线程与轮次均已核对。探针的等待工具调用发生在放行之后，时序结论来自实际观察到的先后。
+- 本次派发正文在宿主输入中以非明文形式记录。初版探针按输入内容匹配标记时断言失败，读回改按宿主任务名、子线程 ID 和原生父子关系核验。正式接入需沿同样的可核实身份关联命名回调。
+
+本次只覆盖一个主题明确的合成首轮。空标题、重试、目录重命名和链接更新留待各自验收。临时项目仍使用会话 ID 主记录路径。 [时序、回传及安装文件校验](../../analysis/acceptance/recording-title-first-prompt.json)
*** End Patch`));

## 工具结果 `exec-0826bf61-c12b-412a-ac54-78c1a6d68e20`

- 来源定位：字节 10213026

~~~text
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/raw/2026-11-27-recording-title-trigger.md:1:# 首次自动命名的触发时机
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md:44:## 2026-11-27 首条落盘后派发命名子代理
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md:46:用户确认首条原话保存后即可尝试命名。命名子代理根据已保存的早期发言返回标题或空值。先验证派发与正常回复可以独立完成，再接入目录改名。 [用户确认](../raw/2026-11-27-recording-title-trigger.md)
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:129:11. 给定已用会话 ID 临时建档的会话，首条原话保存后即可派发命名子代理，由其根据已保存的早期发言判断主题并返回短标题，主代理照常发出回复。在原开启日目录下用采纳的标题重命名会话目录，并同步更新受影响的记录链接。有详情与附件时，主记录仍能打开对应详情，详情仍能打开原附件。后续对话即使换了主题也不再次派发命名，恢复时在现有目录续写，不另建主记录。
specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md:203:- **FR-003** 记录流程在实际保存时根据项目规则和已核实的会话位置选择或复核资料根。首次选址依次考虑项目自定义规则、Agent 工具的实际项目内资料目录、根据项目情况自选的项目内记录目录。主记录、各组详情文件和会话附件均纳入项目 Git 管理，随项目变更提交，并遵守[详情文件与附件](#详情文件与附件)中的提交边界。Git 忽略规则不得覆盖新写入的记录或附件。每个会话按实际开启的本地日期建档；新增布局在日期目录下按对话标题命名会话目录，目录内包含主记录、详情和附件。首次记录以会话 ID 临时命名会话目录，不等待标题，按 FR-002 保存并读回首条消息。首条原话保存后即可启动首次命名，由命名子代理根据已保存的早期发言判断主题并返回短标题或空值。后续有新发言落盘时可再次评估。采纳标题后，在原日期目录下用标题重命名会话目录，并同步更新受影响的记录链接，保留已保存内容及会话 ID。主题不明确或未取得可采纳的标题时，保留会话 ID 目录并继续记录；命名不阻塞正常记录。成功采纳后不因后续对话变化再次自动派发命名，仍适用同名后缀和用户在 Codex 界面主动改名的规则。同一日期目录下，对话标题相同时，所有同名会话的目录名均在标题后添加彼此可区分的短后缀，包括先建档的会话；改名时同步更新受影响的记录链接，不覆盖任何记录。本次澄清不迁移或改写既有记录。会话 ID、实际开启时间和首次工作目录继续作为现有身份及归属核验材料，是否调整其保存方式待目录方案讨论。连续交互和正常恢复跨日均沿合规的资料根和原开启日续写同一份主记录；目录已改名时使用新路径。并行会话各自保存。此次开发变更完成时，移除旧索引版生成的记录产物，建档和续写统一采用经确认的新规则。
真实模型原始观测重新核对通过，证据与已保存文件一致。

~~~

const d=load("title_probe_root");
const cmd=String.raw`set -e
/usr/bin/python3 -B PROBE_DIRECTORY/audit_title_probe.py PROBE_DIRECTORY packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording | cmp - specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/acceptance/recording-title-first-prompt.json
git diff --check -- specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/spec.md specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md
rg -n '^## 2026-11-27 首条|首条原话保存后即可|^# 首次自动命名' specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/{spec.md,requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md,requirements/raw/2026-11-27-recording-title-trigger.md}
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,re
root=Path('specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill')
files=[root/'requirements/raw/2026-11-27-recording-title-trigger.md',
       root/'requirements/discussions/2026-11-26-recording-title-source-research.md',
       root/'spec.md']
for path in files:
 text=path.read_text()
 for rel in re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+?)(?:#[^)]*)?\)',text):
  if 'recording-title-first-prompt.json' in rel or '2026-11-27-recording-title-trigger.md' in rel:
   assert (path.parent/rel).is_file(), rel
data=json.loads((root/'analysis/acceptance/recording-title-first-prompt.json').read_text())
assert data['parentReply']['visibleBeforeChildResult'] and data['parentReply']['finalAnswerCount']==1
assert data['probeChecks']['completedRealModelSamples']==1
print('真实模型原始观测重新核对通过，证据与已保存文件一致。')
PY`;
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:cmd.replaceAll("PROBE_DIRECTORY",d),max_output_tokens:3600});text(r);
